\lxSVG@closescope {{
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{}}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}

Let us now prove this by an explicit computation, which boils down to matrix multiplication. Proposition
6.2.10
implies

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{R_{2}}\circ\hat{f}_{R_{1}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}B\\
B^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}}\circ\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A\\
A^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}B\\
B^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}}\circ\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,(\mu_{A}\otimes\mu_{A^{\prime}})\otimes\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}BA\\
B^{\prime}A\end{bmatrix}}\circ\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k}}\otimes\left((z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\,\circ\,\mu_{A^{\prime}}\right)\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,\left(\mu_{BA}\otimes\mu_{B^{\prime}A}\otimes\mu_{A^{\prime}}\right)\,\circ\,\left(\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}\otimes\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes s}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}.

From Corollary
6.2.11
and Proposition
6.1.2
, we obtain:

\left(\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}\otimes\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes s}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}=\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}&0\\
I_{s}&0\\
0&I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}=\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}.

Thus we have:

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{R_{2}}\circ\hat{f}_{R_{1}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,\left(\mu_{BA}\otimes\mu_{B^{\prime}A}\otimes\mu_{A^{\prime}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}I_{s}\\
I_{s}\\
I_{s}\end{bmatrix}}

\displaystyle=\;\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes l}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}+d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}BA\\
B^{\prime}A\\
A^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}}=\hat{f}_{R_{2}\star R_{1}}

where the equality between the two lines follows from Proposition
6.1.2
. The proposition is proved.

∎

Lemma 9.4.5
.

Let
R_{1}\in Rel^{\infty}_{s_{1},k_{1}}
and
R_{2}\in Rel^{\infty}_{s_{2},k_{2}}
. We have:
\hat{f}_{R_{1}}\otimes\hat{f}_{R_{2}}=\hat{f}_{R_{1}\times R_{2}}
.

Proof.

Let
d_{i}=\dim_{{\mathbb{F}}_{q}}R_{i}
,
r_{i}:=s_{i}+k_{i}
for
i=1,2
.

Let
A^{(i)}\in Mat_{k_{i}\times s_{i}}({\mathbb{F}}_{q}),(A^{\prime})^{(i)}\in Mat_{d_{i}\times s_{i}}({\mathbb{F}}_{q})
be such that

R_{i}=Row\begin{bmatrix}-A^{(i)}&I_{k_{i}}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(i)}&0\end{bmatrix}\;\;rk\left((A^{\prime})^{(i)}\right)=d_{i}.

Consider
R_{1}\times R_{2}
as a subset
{\mathbb{F}}_{q}^{s_{1}+s_{2}+k_{1}+k_{2}}
.
Then

R_{1}\times R_{2}=Row\begin{bmatrix}-A^{(1)}&0&I_{k_{1}}&0\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0&0&0\\
0&-A^{(2)}&0&I_{k_{2}}\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}&0&0\end{bmatrix}=Row\begin{bmatrix}-A^{(1)}&0&I_{k_{1}}&0\\
0&-A^{(2)}&0&I_{k_{2}}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0&0&0\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}&0&0\end{bmatrix}

so

\hat{f}_{R_{1}\times R_{2}}=\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{1}+k_{2}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}+d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}&0\\
0&A^{(2)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}.

Let us compute
\hat{f}_{R_{1}}\otimes\hat{f}_{R_{2}}
. We have:

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{R_{1}}\otimes\hat{f}_{R_{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{1}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}\end{bmatrix}}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{2}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(2)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{1}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\otimes\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{2}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\left(\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}\end{bmatrix}}\otimes\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(2)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}\right)

By Proposition
6.1.2
, we have:

\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}\end{bmatrix}}\otimes\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(2)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}=\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}&0\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0\\
0&A^{(2)}\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}

Let
\sigma:\mathbf{V}^{\otimes d_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{2}}\to\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{V}^{\otimes d_{1}}
be the symmetry morphism. Then
\sigma^{2}=\operatorname{Id}
, so

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{R_{1}}\otimes\hat{f}_{R_{2}}=\left(\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{1}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}}\otimes\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbf{V}^{\otimes k_{2}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\sigma\,\circ\sigma\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}&0\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0\\
0&A^{(2)}\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}

\displaystyle=\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\operatorname{Id}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}+d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\sigma\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}&0\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0\\
0&A^{(2)}\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}=\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\operatorname{Id}}}\otimes(z^{*})^{\otimes d_{1}+d_{2}}\right)\,\circ\,\mu_{\begin{bmatrix}A^{(1)}&0\\
0&A^{(2)}\\
(A^{\prime})^{(1)}&0\\
0&(A^{\prime})^{(2)}\end{bmatrix}}

(the last equality follows from the fact that
\sigma
is a natural transformation). The statement is thus proved.
∎
